    - heading "Controlling decisions" [level=2]
    - heading "Expedition clock, work and provisions" [level=3]
    - paragraph:
      - text: The verdict retains the
      - strong: 12-character Expedition cap
      - text: and establishes one elapsed-time history for participants, work, travel, rests, resource consumption and scheduled events. A team assignment does not grant additional labor, duplicate assistance or an overlapping beneficial rest.
    - paragraph:
      - text: Travel’s
      - strong: 480-minute nominal tranche
      - text: is distinguished from its route- and pace-adjusted elapsed duration. Crafting, training, research, treatment and other explicitly timed procedures retain their actual required work. Completed Strain, Rest Debt, healing and class-resource rules remain controlling rather than being replaced by older Loop summaries.
    - paragraph:
      - strong: NEW Supply authoring
      - text: "defines a physical procurement basket: two pounds of ration-equivalent food, eight pints of potable water, one pint of lamp oil in its flask, and a quarter-pound of ordinary camp sundries. At the selected low-tier source prices, the refill reference is"
      - strong: 63 cp and 11.25 lb
      - text: ", excluding drinking-water containers. Two new waterskins bring the initial portable reference to"
      - strong: 103 cp and 13.25 lb
      - text: .
    - paragraph:
      - text: That basket is
      - strong: not a requirement to burn unused oil or discard sound stock every day
      - text: . Actual food, water, lighting and procedural expenditures have separate, nonduplicating records. Forage, camps, purchases and magical support cannot manufacture stock merely by awarding an abstract Supply label.
    - heading "Pressure, complications, extraction and retreat" [level=3]
    - paragraph: Pressure now has a single cause and event history. Splitting teams, changing the record ID or returning through the same checkpoint does not reset that history or create another reward.
    - paragraph: "The register supplies a complete complication-severity rule, bounded secondary extraction scheduling, finite route checkpoints, participant-budget arithmetic and a retreat procedure. It also resolves the partial-Watch exit boundary: exposure due before reaching safety is settled once, without later inventing a patrol against a party already inside Haven."
    - paragraph: These are bounded authoring choices, not claims that an actual route is mapped, a camp is safe or a particular enemy encounter is balanced.
    - heading "All transport options" [level=3]
    - paragraph:
      - strong: "Every one of the 29 original transport rows is covered:"
      - text: nine mounts, fourteen tack/drawn-vehicle/service entries and six waterborne entries. The catalogue preserves the actual source prices, speeds, carrying figures and missing/null fields. Added creature statistics, body dimensions, crew requirements, capacities, handling, damage, repair and service procedures are explicitly
      - strong: NEW
      - text: .
    - paragraph:
      - text: Mounts, class companions, crafted devices and vehicles remain separate rules objects. For ordinary controlled mounts, the chosen procedure gives the mount
      - strong: one complete turn immediately after the rider’s complete turn
      - text: ", rather than permitting interleaved movement or an extra attack sequence."
    - paragraph:
      - text: The class-interface register also identifies the narrow
      - strong: NEW interpretation of Dragoon’s separate twenty-foot-before-Weave clause
      - text: ": voluntary self-propelled movement qualifies; merely being carried does not. Its original wording and distinct fifteen-foot Momentum procedure remain preserved. Pending Cavalry/Mounted Combatant comparisons receive no approval through this decision."
    - heading "Reputation and the Cult Escapee conflict" [level=3]
    - paragraph:
      - strong: Adopt the NEW −3 through +3 standing policy with five descriptive bands
      - text: ", without an automatic standing or alignment modifier to d20 rolls. The competing eleven-band source and Player-modifier source remain preserved, with their differences explicitly recorded."
    - paragraph: "The Cult Escapee discrepancy is resolved as follows:"
    - paragraph:
      - strong: "For fresh initialization, the complete background feature controls: the applicable suspicious religious authority starts Disliked/−1, not the runtime map’s +1."
    - paragraph:
      - text: An existing save is
      - strong: not
      - text: reset to −1. Where the source version and history establish an erroneous initial contribution, the correction replaces that initialization and then replays the actual earned events. For example, an erroneous +1 start followed by an earned +1 becomes
      - strong: −1 + 1 = 0
      - text: ", rather than deleting the earned change."
    - paragraph:
      - text: Unknown keys, unrecognized versions, absence, null, raw values and history remain intact. No real saves were accessed or migrated. The package retains all
      - strong: 20 background bindings, 44 faction definitions and twelve supplied preservation probes
      - text: .
    - heading "Ordinary social scenes and faction turns" [level=3]
    - paragraph:
      - strong: Meeting the declared social DC earns the declared successful result.
      - text: A costly agreement is a separately declared, optional branch, not an undisclosed extra charge imposed after a successful roll.
    - paragraph: The verdict completes ordinary scene timing, outside-initiative Help, unchanged-premise retries, communication requirements, witnesses and standing-event accounting. Social checks do not confer magical control, legal authority, universal truth or consent.
    - paragraph:
      - text: The

      - paragraph: "KEEP ordinary assay fees at 1% of the face value of the actual accepted assay bill, minimum 1 gp for that real service, and remint fees at 2% of the recovered legitimate metal value accepted for actual reminting. A combined commissioned assay bill receives its minimum once, not per coin, variant or thirty-minute stage; services not incurred create no fee, and an already-owned private testing toolkit creates no fictional desk fee to its owner. KEEP the Loop greater-of rule for a holder lawfully established to be knowing, not a 10% cap. NEW explicit total-payment and fee allocation: let F be the confirmed false lot's face value, A the actual assay fee, R the actual remint fee, and S=A+R for the same assessed lot/bill; the total monetary liability is P=max(0.10×F,S), allocated to service fees S and an additional criminal fine of P−S=max(0,0.10×F−S). Do not charge P plus those fees or levy another 10% fine on top. Compute on exact amounts under FL-21/EC-10's existing final-transaction copper rounding; allocating the total between fees and fine does not itself create extra transactions. Credit actual prior payments and fees already withheld from returned legitimate metal once against the same liability, leaving only its unpaid balance. Confirmed counterfeit coin or plated false currency is seized in full only through actual lawful authority; seizure is neither a fee nor a payment credit. Genuine coins and legitimate metal remain separately identified and are returned with actual fee deductions accounted for once. An innocent holder with documented provenance pays no criminal fine; absent a lawful knowing-holder finding, this rule imposes no criminal fine. Suspicion, ancestry, a failed die or missing documentation alone does not establish knowledge. Assessment, collection and custody require actual authorized actors; an unpaid amount remains an obligation, not automatic borrowing or remote confiscation. Cutting/reminting takes the provider's actual work and links consumed/replaced coin identities to real metal/output quantities without duplication."
